Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the relativity object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 117. The moduli space has dimension at most 431 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_117(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 117. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^431(S, F) is trivial. QED.
